### FILE 4: `internal/provider/npm/npm.go` - TEMPLATE FOR NEW PROVIDERS
**Location:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/docs/registries/internal/provider/npm/npm.go`

**FULL CONTENT - 254 LINES:**

```go
package npm

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"net/http"
	"net/url"
	"regexp"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider"
)

var validPkgRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^(@[a-zA-Z0-9][\w.-]*/)?[a-zA-Z0-9][\w.-]*$`)

const (
	defaultRegistryURL  = "https://registry.npmjs.org"
	defaultDownloadsURL = "https://api.npmjs.org"
)

// Provider fetches metrics from the npm registry.
type Provider struct {
	registryURL  string
	downloadsURL string
	client       *http.Client
}

// New creates an npm provider. Pass empty strings for default URLs.
func New(registryURL, downloadsURL string) *Provider {
	if registryURL == "" {
		registryURL = defaultRegistryURL
	}
	if downloadsURL == "" {
		downloadsURL = defaultDownloadsURL
	}
	return &Provider{
		registryURL:  strings.TrimRight(registryURL, "/"),
		downloadsURL: strings.TrimRight(downloadsURL, "/"),
		client:       &http.Client{Timeout: 30 * time.Second},
	}
}

func (p *Provider) Scheme() string { return "npm" }

func (p *Provider) Fetch(ctx context.Context, identifier string) (provider.Result, error) {
	if identifier == "" || !validPkgRe.MatchString(identifier) {
		return provider.Result{
			Target: "npm:" + identifier,
			Error:  fmt.Sprintf("invalid npm package name %q", identifier),
		}, nil
	}

	metrics := &provider.NPMMetrics{}
	var mu sync.Mutex
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	var errs []string

	type job struct {
		name string
		fn   func(context.Context) error
	}

	jobs := []job{
		{"latest", func(ctx context.Context) error {
			latest, err := p.fetchLatest(ctx, identifier)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			mu.Lock()
			metrics.LatestVersion = latest.Version
			metrics.DependenciesCount = len(latest.Dependencies)
			metrics.License = latest.License
			mu.Unlock()
			return nil
		}},
		{"modified", func(ctx context.Context) error {
			days, err := p.fetchLastPublishDays(ctx, identifier)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			mu.Lock()
			metrics.LastPublishDays = days
			mu.Unlock()
			return nil
		}},
		{"downloads", func(ctx context.Context) error {
			count, err := p.fetchWeeklyDownloads(ctx, identifier)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			mu.Lock()
			metrics.WeeklyDownloads = count
			mu.Unlock()
			return nil
		}},
	}

	wg.Add(len(jobs))
	for _, j := range jobs {
		go func(j job) {
			defer wg.Done()
			if err := j.fn(ctx); err != nil {
				mu.Lock()
				errs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s", j.name, err.Error()))
				mu.Unlock()
			}
		}(j)
	}
	wg.Wait()

	result := provider.Result{
		Target: "npm:" + identifier,
	}

	if len(errs) == len(jobs) {
		result.Error = strings.Join(errs, "; ")
		return result, nil
	}

	result.NPM = metrics
	if len(errs) > 0 {
		result.Error = strings.Join(errs, "; ")
	}
	return result, nil
}

type latestResponse struct {
	Version      string            `json:"version"`
	Dependencies map[string]string `json:"dependencies"`
	License      string
	RawLicense   json.RawMessage `json:"license"`
}

func (p *Provider) fetchLatest(ctx context.Context, pkg string) (*latestResponse, error) {
	u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s/latest", p.registryURL, pkg)
	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, "GET", u, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	resp, err := p.client.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()

	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("npm registry: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, http.StatusText(resp.StatusCode))
	}

	var latest latestResponse
	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&latest); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("decoding response: %w", err)
	}

	latest.License = parseLicense(latest.RawLicense)
	return &latest, nil
}

func parseLicense(raw json.RawMessage) string {
	if len(raw) == 0 {
		return ""
	}

	var s string
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &s); err == nil {
		return s
	}

	var obj struct {
		Type string `json:"type"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &obj); err == nil {
		return obj.Type
	}
	return ""
}

func (p *Provider) fetchLastPublishDays(ctx context.Context, pkg string) (int, error) {
	u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s", p.registryURL, pkg)
	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, "GET", u, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/vnd.npm.install-v1+json")

	resp, err := p.client.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()

	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("npm registry: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, http.StatusText(resp.StatusCode))
	}

	var meta struct {
		Modified string `json:"modified"`
	}
	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&meta); err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("decoding response: %w", err)
	}

	t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, meta.Modified)
	if err != nil {
		t, err = time.Parse("2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z", meta.Modified)
		if err != nil {
			return 0, fmt.Errorf("parsing modified date %q: %w", meta.Modified, err)
		}
	}

	days := int(math.Floor(time.Since(t).Hours() / 24))
	if days < 0 {
		days = 0
	}
	return days, nil
}

func (p *Provider) fetchWeeklyDownloads(ctx context.Context, pkg string) (int, error) {
	// For scoped packages (@scope/name), the npm downloads API requires
	// the package name as a single path token with the slash encoded as %2F
	// (e.g., @scope%2Fname). We use url.PathEscape on the full name to
	// achieve this, since PathEscape encodes "/" to %2F.
	encodedPkg := url.PathEscape(pkg)

	u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/downloads/point/last-week/%s", p.downloadsURL, encodedPkg)
	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, "GET", u, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}

	resp, err := p.client.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()

	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("npm downloads API: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, http.StatusText(resp.StatusCode))
	}

	var dl struct {
		Downloads int `json:"downloads"`
	}
	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&dl); err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("decoding response: %w", err)
	}
	return dl.Downloads, nil
}
```

**KEY PATTERNS FOR NEW PROVIDERS:**
1. **Lines 26-30**: Provider struct with HTTP client and URLs
2. **Lines 32-45**: New() constructor with default URLs and HTTP client (30s timeout)
3. **Line 47**: Scheme() method returning string literal (e.g., "npm")
4. **Lines 49-129**: Fetch() method structure:
   - Input validation (lines 50-55)
   - Create metrics pointer (line 57)
   - Mutex + WaitGroup for parallel jobs (lines 58-60)
   - Jobs array with error handling (lines 62-100)
   - Result assembly with error joining (lines 102-128)
5. **Lines 131-162**: Helper fetch methods using HTTP + JSON parsing
6. **Error handling**: Partial failure support (line 119-127)

```go
package npm

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func mustEncode(w http.ResponseWriter, v any) {
	if err := json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(v); err != nil {
		panic(err)
	}
}

func setupMockServers(t *testing.T) (registry *httptest.Server, downloads *httptest.Server) {
	t.Helper()

	regMux := http.NewServeMux()

	// GET /react/latest
	regMux.HandleFunc("GET /react/latest", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{
			"name":    "react",
			"version": "19.1.0",
			"license": "MIT",
			"dependencies": map[string]any{
				"loose-envify":    "^1.1.0",
				"object-assign":   "^4.1.1",
			},
		})
	})

	// GET /react (abbreviated metadata)
	modified15d := time.Now().Add(-15 * 24 * time.Hour).Format("2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z")
	regMux.HandleFunc("GET /react", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		if r.Header.Get("Accept") != "application/vnd.npm.install-v1+json" {
			w.WriteHeader(http.StatusBadRequest)
			return
		}
		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{
			"modified": modified15d,
		})
	})

	dlMux := http.NewServeMux()

	// GET /downloads/point/last-week/react
	dlMux.HandleFunc("GET /downloads/point/last-week/react", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{
			"downloads": 25000000,
			"package":   "react",
		})
	})

	registry = httptest.NewServer(regMux)
	downloads = httptest.NewServer(dlMux)
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		registry.Close()
		downloads.Close()
	})
	return
}

func TestScheme(t *testing.T) {
	p := New("", "")
	if p.Scheme() != "npm" {
		t.Errorf("got %q, want %q", p.Scheme(), "npm")
	}
}

func TestFetchSuccess(t *testing.T) {
	reg, dl := setupMockServers(t)
	p := New(reg.URL, dl.URL)

	result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), "react")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
	}
	if result.Error != "" {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected result error: %s", result.Error)
	}
	if result.Target != "npm:react" {
		t.Errorf("target: got %q, want %q", result.Target, "npm:react")
	}
	n := result.NPM
	if n == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected NPM metrics to be set")
	}
	if n.WeeklyDownloads != 25000000 {
		t.Errorf("weekly_downloads: got %d, want 25000000", n.WeeklyDownloads)
	}
	if n.LatestVersion != "19.1.0" {
		t.Errorf("latest_version: got %q, want %q", n.LatestVersion, "19.1.0")
	}
	if n.DependenciesCount != 2 {
		t.Errorf("dependencies_count: got %d, want 2", n.DependenciesCount)
	}
	if n.License != "MIT" {
		t.Errorf("license: got %q, want %q", n.License, "MIT")
	}
	if n.LastPublishDays < 14 || n.LastPublishDays > 16 {
		t.Errorf("last_publish_days: got %d, want ~15", n.LastPublishDays)
	}
}

func TestFetchNotFound(t *testing.T) {
	regMux := http.NewServeMux()
	regMux.HandleFunc("GET /nonexistent-pkg/latest", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNotFound)
	})
	regMux.HandleFunc("GET /nonexistent-pkg", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNotFound)
	})
	reg := httptest.NewServer(regMux)
	defer reg.Close()

	dlMux := http.NewServeMux()
	dlMux.HandleFunc("GET /downloads/point/last-week/nonexistent-pkg", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNotFound)
	})
	dl := httptest.NewServer(dlMux)
	defer dl.Close()

	p := New(reg.URL, dl.URL)
	result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), "nonexistent-pkg")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected Go error: %v", err)
	}
	if result.Error == "" {
		t.Fatal("expected result.Error to be set for 404")
	}
	if result.NPM != nil {
		t.Error("expected NPM to be nil on full error")
	}
}

func TestFetchEmptyIdentifier(t *testing.T) {
	p := New("http://unused", "http://unused")
	result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), "")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected Go error: %v", err)
	}
	if result.Error == "" {
		t.Fatal("expected result.Error for empty identifier")
	}
}

func TestFetchScopedPackage(t *testing.T) {
	regMux := http.NewServeMux()
	regMux.HandleFunc("GET /@types/node/latest", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{
			"name":         "@types/node",
			"version":      "22.13.0",
			"license":      "MIT",
			"dependencies": map[string]any{"undici-types": "~6.20.0"},
		})
	})
	regMux.HandleFunc("GET /@types/node", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		if r.Header.Get("Accept") != "application/vnd.npm.install-v1+json" {
			w.WriteHeader(http.StatusBadRequest)
			return
		}
		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{
			"modified": time.Now().Add(-5 * 24 * time.Hour).Format("2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z"),
		})
	})
	reg := httptest.NewServer(regMux)
	defer reg.Close()

	dlMux := http.NewServeMux()
	// scoped package: url.PathEscape encodes "/" as %2F, which Go's ServeMux
	// doesn't decode for pattern matching. Use a wildcard to match.
	dlMux.HandleFunc("GET /downloads/point/last-week/{pkg...}", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{"downloads": 5000000})
	})
	dl := httptest.NewServer(dlMux)
	defer dl.Close()

	p := New(reg.URL, dl.URL)
	result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), "@types/node")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
	}
	if result.Error != "" {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected result error: %s", result.Error)
	}
	if result.NPM == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected NPM metrics to be set")
	}
	if result.NPM.LatestVersion != "22.13.0" {
		t.Errorf("latest_version: got %q, want %q", result.NPM.LatestVersion, "22.13.0")
	}
	if result.NPM.WeeklyDownloads != 5000000 {
		t.Errorf("weekly_downloads: got %d, want 5000000", result.NPM.WeeklyDownloads)
	}
}

func TestFetchLicenseObject(t *testing.T) {
	regMux := http.NewServeMux()
	regMux.HandleFunc("GET /old-pkg/latest", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		// license as object (legacy format)
		_, _ = w.Write([]byte(`{"name":"old-pkg","version":"1.0.0","license":{"type":"ISC","url":"https://example.com"},"dependencies":{}}`))
	})
	regMux.HandleFunc("GET /old-pkg", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		if r.Header.Get("Accept") != "application/vnd.npm.install-v1+json" {
			w.WriteHeader(http.StatusBadRequest)
			return
		}
		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{"modified": time.Now().Add(-55 * 24 * time.Hour).Format("2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z")})
	})
	reg := httptest.NewServer(regMux)
	defer reg.Close()

	dlMux := http.NewServeMux()
	dlMux.HandleFunc("GET /downloads/point/last-week/old-pkg", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{"downloads": 100})
	})
	dl := httptest.NewServer(dlMux)
	defer dl.Close()

	p := New(reg.URL, dl.URL)
	result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), "old-pkg")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
	}
	if result.Error != "" {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected result error: %s", result.Error)
	}
	if result.NPM.License != "ISC" {
		t.Errorf("license: got %q, want %q", result.NPM.License, "ISC")
	}
}

func TestFetchNoDependencies(t *testing.T) {
	regMux := http.NewServeMux()
	regMux.HandleFunc("GET /no-deps/latest", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		// no dependencies field at all
		_, _ = w.Write([]byte(`{"name":"no-deps","version":"1.0.0","license":"MIT"}`))
	})
	regMux.HandleFunc("GET /no-deps", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		if r.Header.Get("Accept") != "application/vnd.npm.install-v1+json" {
			w.WriteHeader(http.StatusBadRequest)
			return
		}
		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{"modified": time.Now().Add(-24 * 24 * time.Hour).Format("2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z")})
	})
	reg := httptest.NewServer(regMux)
	defer reg.Close()

	dlMux := http.NewServeMux()
	dlMux.HandleFunc("GET /downloads/point/last-week/no-deps", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{"downloads": 500})
	})
	dl := httptest.NewServer(dlMux)
	defer dl.Close()

	p := New(reg.URL, dl.URL)
	result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), "no-deps")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
	}
	if result.NPM.DependenciesCount != 0 {
		t.Errorf("dependencies_count: got %d, want 0", result.NPM.DependenciesCount)
	}
}

func TestFetchPartialFailure(t *testing.T) {
	regMux := http.NewServeMux()
	regMux.HandleFunc("GET /partial/latest", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{
			"name":    "partial",
			"version": "2.0.0",
			"license": "Apache-2.0",
		})
	})
	regMux.HandleFunc("GET /partial", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		if r.Header.Get("Accept") != "application/vnd.npm.install-v1+json" {
			w.WriteHeader(http.StatusBadRequest)
			return
		}
		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{"modified": time.Now().Add(-10 * 24 * time.Hour).Format("2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z")})
	})
	reg := httptest.NewServer(regMux)
	defer reg.Close()

	// downloads API returns 500
	dlMux := http.NewServeMux()
	dlMux.HandleFunc("GET /downloads/point/last-week/partial", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusInternalServerError)
	})
	dl := httptest.NewServer(dlMux)
	defer dl.Close()

	p := New(reg.URL, dl.URL)
	result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), "partial")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected Go error: %v", err)
	}
	// Should have partial metrics
	if result.NPM == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected NPM metrics for partial failure")
	}
	if result.NPM.LatestVersion != "2.0.0" {
		t.Errorf("latest_version: got %q, want %q", result.NPM.LatestVersion, "2.0.0")
	}
	// Should also have error
	if result.Error == "" {
		t.Fatal("expected result.Error for partial failure")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(result.Error, "downloads") {
		t.Errorf("expected error to mention downloads, got %q", result.Error)
	}
}

**RELEVANT SECTION - FULL FILE (153 lines):**

```go
package cli

import (
	"context"
	"flag"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/auth"
	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/cache"
	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/format"
	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider"
	ghprovider "github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider/github"
	npmprovider "github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider/npm"
)

// Version is set at build time via ldflags.
var Version = "dev"

const timeout = 30 * time.Second

// Run executes the CLI with the given arguments.
func Run(args []string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) error {
	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("repiq", flag.ContinueOnError)
	fs.SetOutput(stderr)

	jsonFlag := fs.Bool("json", false, "output as JSON array (default)")
	ndjsonFlag := fs.Bool("ndjson", false, "output as newline-delimited JSON")
	markdownFlag := fs.Bool("markdown", false, "output as Markdown table")
	versionFlag := fs.Bool("version", false, "print version and exit")
	noCacheFlag := fs.Bool("no-cache", false, "bypass cache and always fetch from API")

	fs.Usage = func() {
		_, _ = io.WriteString(stderr, `Usage: repiq [flags] <scheme>:<identifier> [...]

Fetch objective metrics for OSS libraries and repositories.

Examples:
  repiq github:facebook/react
  repiq npm:react
  repiq --ndjson github:facebook/react npm:react
  repiq --markdown github:golang/go

Flags:
`)
		fs.PrintDefaults()
	}

	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if *versionFlag {
		_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "repiq %s\n", Version)
		return nil
	}

	targets := fs.Args()
	if len(targets) == 0 {
		fs.Usage()
		return fmt.Errorf("no targets specified")
	}

	// Determine output format. When multiple flags are set, priority:
	// markdown > ndjson > json (default). This matches the spec's
	// "last specified wins" intent for wrapper scripts that set defaults.
	formatter := format.JSON
	if *ndjsonFlag {
		formatter = format.NDJSON
	}
	if *markdownFlag {
		formatter = format.Markdown
	}
	if *jsonFlag && !*ndjsonFlag && !*markdownFlag {
		formatter = format.JSON
	}

	// Set up providers.
	resolver := &auth.Resolver{
		Cmd:    auth.ExecRunner{},
		Getenv: os.Getenv,
	}
	token := resolver.ResolveToken()

	registry := provider.NewRegistry()

	ghProvider := provider.Provider(ghprovider.New(token, ""))
	npmProvider := provider.Provider(npmprovider.New("", ""))

	if cacheDir, err := os.UserCacheDir(); err == nil {
		store := cache.NewStore(filepath.Join(cacheDir, "repiq"), 24*time.Hour)
		ghProvider = cache.NewProvider(ghProvider, store, *noCacheFlag)
		npmProvider = cache.NewProvider(npmProvider, store, *noCacheFlag)
	}

	registry.Register(ghProvider)
	registry.Register(npmProvider)

	// Parse and validate all targets first.
	parsed := make([]provider.Target, len(targets))
	for i, raw := range targets {
		t, err := provider.ParseTarget(raw)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if _, ok := registry.Lookup(t.Scheme); !ok {
			return fmt.Errorf("unknown scheme %q in target %q", t.Scheme, raw)
		}
		parsed[i] = t
	}

	// Fetch all targets in parallel.
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), timeout)
	defer cancel()

	results := make([]provider.Result, len(parsed))
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	wg.Add(len(parsed))
	for i, t := range parsed {
		go func(i int, t provider.Target) {
			defer wg.Done()
			p, _ := registry.Lookup(t.Scheme)
			result, err := p.Fetch(ctx, t.Identifier)
			if err != nil {
				results[i] = provider.Result{
					Target: t.Scheme + ":" + t.Identifier,
					Error:  err.Error(),
				}
				return
			}
			results[i] = result
		}(i, t)
	}
	wg.Wait()

	// Output results.
	if err := formatter(stdout, results); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("formatting output: %w", err)
	}

	// Exit code: 1 if any result has an error.
	for _, r := range results {
		if r.Error != "" {
			return fmt.Errorf("one or more targets failed")
		}
	}
	return nil
}
```

**FULL FILE (133 lines):**

```go
package format

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider"
)

func escapeMarkdown(s string) string {
	return strings.ReplaceAll(s, "|", "\\|")
}

// JSON writes results as a JSON array.
func JSON(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result) error {
	if results == nil {
		results = []provider.Result{}
	}
	enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
	enc.SetIndent("", "  ")
	return enc.Encode(results)
}

// NDJSON writes results as newline-delimited JSON (one object per line).
func NDJSON(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result) error {
	enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
	for _, r := range results {
		if err := enc.Encode(r); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// Markdown writes results as a Markdown table grouped by scheme.
func Markdown(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result) error {
	var ghResults []provider.Result
	var npmResults []provider.Result
	var errResults []provider.Result
	for _, r := range results {
		switch {
		case r.GitHub != nil:
			ghResults = append(ghResults, r)
		case r.NPM != nil:
			npmResults = append(npmResults, r)
		default:
			errResults = append(errResults, r)
		}
	}

	needSep := false

	if len(ghResults) > 0 {
		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "| target | stars | forks | open_issues | contributors | release_count | last_commit_days | commits_30d | issues_closed_30d | error |"); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, r := range ghResults {
			g := r.GitHub
			if _, err := fmt.Fprintf(w, "| %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s |\n",
				escapeMarkdown(r.Target),
				strconv.Itoa(g.Stars),
				strconv.Itoa(g.Forks),
				strconv.Itoa(g.OpenIssues),
				strconv.Itoa(g.Contributors),
				strconv.Itoa(g.ReleaseCount),
				strconv.Itoa(g.LastCommitDays),
				strconv.Itoa(g.Commits30d),
				strconv.Itoa(g.IssuesClosed30d),
				escapeMarkdown(r.Error),
			); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		needSep = true
	}

	if len(npmResults) > 0 {
		if needSep {
			if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "| target | weekly_downloads | latest_version | last_publish_days | dependencies_count | license | error |"); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, r := range npmResults {
			n := r.NPM
			if _, err := fmt.Fprintf(w, "| %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s |\n",
				escapeMarkdown(r.Target),
				strconv.Itoa(n.WeeklyDownloads),
				escapeMarkdown(n.LatestVersion),
				strconv.Itoa(n.LastPublishDays),
				strconv.Itoa(n.DependenciesCount),
				escapeMarkdown(n.License),
				escapeMarkdown(r.Error),
			); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		needSep = true
	}

	if len(errResults) > 0 {
		if needSep {
			if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "| target | error |"); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "|---|---|"); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, r := range errResults {
			if _, err := fmt.Fprintf(w, "| %s | %s |\n", escapeMarkdown(r.Target), escapeMarkdown(r.Error)); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	}

	return nil
}
```

### FILE 8: `internal/cache/provider.go` - CACHE DECORATOR
**Location:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/docs/registries/internal/cache/provider.go`

**FULL FILE (53 lines):**

```go
package cache

import (
	"context"

	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider"
)

var _ provider.Provider = (*Provider)(nil)

// Provider wraps a provider.Provider with disk caching.
type Provider struct {
	underlying provider.Provider
	store      *Store
	noCache    bool
}

// NewProvider creates a caching decorator around the given provider.
// When noCache is true, the cache is bypassed on reads but results are
// still written (so subsequent runs without --no-cache benefit).
func NewProvider(underlying provider.Provider, store *Store, noCache bool) *Provider {
	return &Provider{
		underlying: underlying,
		store:      store,
		noCache:    noCache,
	}
}

func (p *Provider) Scheme() string {
	return p.underlying.Scheme()
}

func (p *Provider) Fetch(ctx context.Context, identifier string) (provider.Result, error) {
	key := p.underlying.Scheme() + ":" + identifier

	if !p.noCache {
		if cached, ok := p.store.Get(key); ok {
			return cached, nil
		}
	}

	result, err := p.underlying.Fetch(ctx, identifier)
	if err != nil {
		return result, err
	}

	if result.Error == "" {
		_ = p.store.Set(key, result)
	}

	return result, nil
}
```

**NO CHANGES NEEDED** - Cache decorator is generic and works with any provider.

metadata job: GET /pypi/{package}/json → version, last_publish_days, deps count, license, requires_python\n    - downloads job: GET /api/packages/{package}/recent → weekly/monthly downloads\n    - requires_dist から dependencies_count 算出時、extras 条件 (; extra ==) を含むものを除外\n    - エラーハンドリング: npm パターンに従い、全 job 失敗で metrics=nil、部分失敗で partial result\n  Why: PyPI は Python エコシステムの中核レジストリ。weekly/monthly downloads で利用実態が把握できる\n  Verify: `go test ./internal/provider/pypi/... -v` で以下が pass:\n    - TestScheme\n    - TestFetchSuccess (全 7 メトリクス検証)\n    - TestFetchNotFound (404 → error message)\n    - TestFetchEmptyIdentifier (バリデーション)\n    - TestFetchPartialFailure (downloads API 失敗 → partial result)\n    - TestFetchNullRequiresDist (requires_dist=null → deps=0)\n    - TestFetchExtrasExcluded (extras 依存除外)\n  Files: internal/provider/pypi/pypi.go, internal/provider/pypi/pypi_test.go\n  Depends: #1\n\n- [ ] #3 Implement crates.io provider with tests\n  What: crates.io API からメトリクスを取得するプロバイダーを作成する\n  Where: internal/provider/crates/crates.go, internal/provider/crates/crates_test.go (新規作成)\n  How: npm プロバイダーをテンプレートとして:\n    - Provider 構造体: baseURL + http.Client (User-Agent 付き RoundTripper)\n    - New(baseURL string) コンストラクタ\n    - Scheme() → \"crates\"\n    - Fetch(): identifier バリデーション → 2 段階フロー\n      1. metadata job: GET /api/v1/crates/{crate} → downloads, recent_downloads, latest_version, last_publish_days, license\n         - latest_version: max_stable_version (null なら newest_version)\n         - last_publish_days/license: versions 配列から latest_version に一致するエントリを使用\n      2. (metadata 完了後) dependencies + reverse_deps を並列:\n         - GET /api/v1/crates/{crate}/{version}/dependencies → normal kind のみカウント\n         - GET /api/v1/crates/{crate}/reverse_dependencies?per_page=1 → meta.total\n    - User-Agent: \"repiq/<version> (https://github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq)\"\n      GitHub プロバイダーの tokenTransport パターンを参考にカスタム RoundTripper を実装\n  Why: crates.io は Rust エコシステムの中核。reverse_dependencies はライブラリの普及度を示す重要指標\n  Verify: `go test ./internal/provider/crates/... -v` で以下が pass:\n    - TestScheme\n    - TestFetchSuccess (全 7 メトリクス検証)\n    - TestFetchNotFound (404 → error message)\n    - TestFetchEmptyIdentifier (バリデーション)\n    - TestFetchPartialFailure (deps API 失敗 → partial result)\n    - TestFetchPreReleaseOnly (max_stable_version=null → newest_version)\n    - TestUserAgentHeader (User-Agent ヘッダー検証)\n  Files: internal/provider/crates/crates.go, internal/provider/crates/crates_test.go\n  Depends: #1\n\n- [ ] #4 Implement Go Modules provider with tests\n  What: proxy.golang.org + deps.dev API からメトリクスを取得するプロバイダーを作成する\n  Where: internal/provider/golang/golang.go, internal/provider/golang/golang_test.go (新規作成)\n  How: npm プロバイダーをテンプレートとして:\n    - Provider 構造体: proxyURL + depsdevURL + http.Client\n    - New(proxyURL, depsdevURL string) コンストラクタ\n    - Scheme() → \"go\"\n    - パッケージ名は \"golang\" (Go の予約語回避)\n    - Fetch(): identifier バリデーション (ドメイン含むパス形式) → 2 段階フロー\n      1. proxy job: GET /{escaped_module}/@latest → latest_version, last_publish_days\n         - モジュールパスエスケープ: 大文字を !+小文字 に変換 (Go proxy 規則)\n      2. (proxy 完了後) deps.dev の 2 エンドポイントを並列:\n         - GET /v3alpha/systems/go/packages/{url_encoded}/versions/{version} → license\n         - GET /v3alpha/systems/go/packages/{url_encoded}/versions/{version}:dependencies → deps count (DIRECT のみ)\n    - deps.dev フォールバック: API 失敗時は deps_count=0, license=\"\" + partial error\n  Why: Go は AI エージェントが頻繁に扱う言語。ダウンロード数は取得不可だが version/deps/license は有用\n  Verify: `go test ./internal/provider/golang/... -v` で以下が pass:\n    - TestScheme\n    - TestFetchSuccess (全 4 メトリクス検証)\n    - TestFetchNotFound (proxy 404 → error message)\n    - TestFetchEmptyIdentifier (バリデーション)\n    - TestFetchDepsDevFailure (deps.dev 失敗 → partial result with version info)\n    - TestModulePathEscape (大文字モジュールパスのエスケープ)\n    - TestMajorVersionSuffix (v2 サフィックス付きパス)\n  Files: internal/provider/golang/golang.go, internal/provider/golang/golang_test.go\n  Depends: #1\n\n- [ ] #5 Add Markdown format tables for new providers with tests\n  What: format.go の Markdown 関数に PyPI / crates.io / Go Modules 用のテーブルレンダリングを追加する\n  Where: internal/format/format.go, internal/format/format_test.go\n  How: 既存の npm テーブルブロック (GitHub/NPM の switch-case + テーブル出力) と同じパターンで:\n    - Markdown() 内の switch に 3 つの case を追加 (r.PyPI != nil, r.Crates != nil, r.Go != nil)\n    - 各プロバイダー用の変数 (pypiResults, cratesResults, goResults) を追加\n    - 各メトリクスのカラムに対応するヘッダー行 + セパレーター行 + データ行\n    - needSep による空行挿入で各テーブルを分離\n  Why: --markdown 出力でユーザーが新プロバイダーのメトリクスを表形式で確認できるようにする\n  Verify: `go test ./internal/format/... -v` で以下が pass:\n    - TestMarkdownPyPI (PyPI メトリクスのテーブル出力)\n    - TestMarkdownCrates (crates メトリクスのテーブル出力)\n    - TestMarkdownGo (Go メトリクスのテーブル出力)\n    - TestMarkdownMixed (全プロバイダー混合出力)\n    - 既存テスト (GitHub, npm) がリグレッションなし\n  Files: internal/format/format.go, internal/format/format_test.go\n  Depends: #1\n\n- [ ] #6 Register new providers in CLI with cache wrapping\n  What: cli.go に 3 つの新プロバイダーをインポート・生成・キャッシュラップ・レジストリ登録する。ヘルプテキストの Examples を更新する\n  Where: internal/cli/cli.go, internal/cli/cli_test.go\n  How: 既存の GitHub/npm 登録パターンに従う:\n    - import に 3 つのプロバイダーパッケージを追加\n    - New() でプロバイダー生成 → cache.NewProvider() でラップ → registry.Register()\n    - fs.Usage の Examples に `repiq pypi:requests`, `repiq crates:serde`, `repiq go:golang.org/x/text` を追加\n  Why: プロバイダーが CLI から使えるようにする最終統合ステップ\n  Verify: `go test ./internal/cli/... -v` が pass。`go build ./cmd/repiq` が成功\n  Files: internal/cli/cli.go, internal/cli/cli_test.go\n  Depends: #2, #3, #4\n\n- [ ] #7 Update README with new provider examples\n  What: README.md に PyPI / crates.io / Go Modules の使用例と対応メトリクス一覧を追加する\n  Where: README.md\n  How: 既存の GitHub/npm セクションと同じフォーマットで:\n    - 使用例: `repiq pypi:requests`, `repiq crates:serde`, `repiq go:golang.org/x/text`\n    - 各プロバイダーのメトリクス一覧表\n    - 混合ターゲットの例を更新\n  Why: ユーザーが新プロバイダーの存在と使い方を把握できるようにする\n  Verify: README に新プロバイダーの例が含まれている。リンク切れなし\n  Files: README.md\n  Depends: #6\n"

    59→- [ ] #2 Implement PyPI provider with tests
    60→  What: PyPI JSON API + pypistats.org からメトリクスを取得するプロバイダーを作成する
    61→  Where: internal/provider/pypi/pypi.go, internal/provider/pypi/pypi_test.go (新規作成)
    62→  How: npm プロバイダー (internal/provider/npm/npm.go) をテンプレートとして:
    63→    - Provider 構造体: pypiURL + statsURL + http.Client
    64→    - New(pypiURL, statsURL string) コンストラクタ (空文字でデフォルト URL)
    65→    - Scheme() → "pypi"
    66→    - Fetch(): identifier バリデーション → 2 job 並列 (metadata + downloads)
    67→    - metadata job: GET /pypi/{package}/json → version, last_publish_days, deps count, license, requires_python
    68→    - downloads job: GET /api/packages/{package}/recent → weekly/monthly downloads
    69→    - requires_dist から dependencies_count 算出時、extras 条件 (; extra ==) を含むものを除外
    70→    - エラーハンドリング: npm パターンに従い、全 job 失敗で metrics=nil、部分失敗で partial result
    71→  Why: PyPI は Python エコシステムの中核レジストリ。weekly/monthly downloads で利用実態が把握できる
    72→  Verify: `go test ./internal/provider/pypi/... -v` で以下が pass:
    73→    - TestScheme
    74→    - TestFetchSuccess (全 7 メトリクス検証)
    75→    - TestFetchNotFound (404 → error message)
    76→    - TestFetchEmptyIdentifier (バリデーション)
    77→    - TestFetchPartialFailure (downloads API 失敗 → partial result)
    78→    - TestFetchNullRequiresDist (requires_dist=null → deps=0)
    79→    - TestFetchExtrasExcluded (extras 依存除外)
    80→  Files: internal/provider/pypi/pypi.go, internal/provider/pypi/pypi_test.go
    81→  Depends: #1

    83→- [ ] #3 Implement crates.io provider with tests
    84→  What: crates.io API からメトリクスを取得するプロバイダーを作成する
    85→  Where: internal/provider/crates/crates.go, internal/provider/crates/crates_test.go (新規作成)
    86→  How: npm プロバイダーをテンプレートとして:
    87→    - Provider 構造体: baseURL + http.Client (User-Agent 付き RoundTripper)
    88→    - New(baseURL string) コンストラクタ
    89→    - Scheme() → "crates"
    90→    - Fetch(): identifier バリデーション → 2 段階フロー
    91→      1. metadata job: GET /api/v1/crates/{crate} → downloads, recent_downloads, latest_version, last_publish_days, license
    92→         - latest_version: max_stable_version (null なら newest_version)
    93→         - last_publish_days/license: versions 配列から latest_version に一致するエントリを使用
    94→      2. (metadata 完了後) dependencies + reverse_deps を並列:
    95→         - GET /api/v1/crates/{crate}/{version}/dependencies → normal kind のみカウント
    96→         - GET /api/v1/crates/{crate}/reverse_dependencies?per_page=1 → meta.total
    97→    - User-Agent: "repiq/<version> (https://github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq)"
    98→      GitHub プロバイダーの tokenTransport パターンを参考にカスタム RoundTripper を実装
    99→  Why: crates.io は Rust エコシステムの中核。reverse_dependencies はライブラリの普及度を示す重要指標
   100→  Verify: `go test ./internal/provider/crates/... -v` で以下が pass:
   101→    - TestScheme
   102→    - TestFetchSuccess (全 7 メトリクス検証)
   103→    - TestFetchNotFound (404 → error message)
   104→    - TestFetchEmptyIdentifier (バリデーション)
   105→    - TestFetchPartialFailure (deps API 失敗 → partial result)
   106→    - TestFetchPreReleaseOnly (max_stable_version=null → newest_version)
   107→    - TestUserAgentHeader (User-Agent ヘッダー検証)
   108→  Files: internal/provider/crates/crates.go, internal/provider/crates/crates_test.go

   111→- [ ] #4 Implement Go Modules provider with tests
   112→  What: proxy.golang.org + deps.dev API からメトリクスを取得するプロバイダーを作成する
   113→  Where: internal/provider/golang/golang.go, internal/provider/golang/golang_test.go (新規作成)
   114→  How: npm プロバイダーをテンプレートとして:
   115→    - Provider 構造体: proxyURL + depsdevURL + http.Client
   116→    - New(proxyURL, depsdevURL string) コンストラクタ
   117→    - Scheme() → "go"
   118→    - パッケージ名は "golang" (Go の予約語回避)
   119→    - Fetch(): identifier バリデーション (ドメイン含むパス形式) → 2 段階フロー
   120→      1. proxy job: GET /{escaped_module}/@latest → latest_version, last_publish_days
   121→         - モジュールパスエスケープ: 大文字を !+小文字 に変換 (Go proxy 規則)
   122→      2. (proxy 完了後) deps.dev の 2 エンドポイントを並列:
   123→         - GET /v3alpha/systems/go/packages/{url_encoded}/versions/{version} → license
   124→         - GET /v3alpha/systems/go/packages/{url_encoded}/versions/{version}:dependencies → deps count (DIRECT のみ)
   125→    - deps.dev フォールバック: API 失敗時は deps_count=0, license="" + partial error
   126→  Why: Go は AI エージェントが頻繁に扱う言語。ダウンロード数は取得不可だが version/deps/license は有用

   132→    - TestFetchDepsDevFailure (deps.dev 失敗 → partial result with version info)
   133→    - TestModulePathEscape (大文字モジュールパスのエスケープ)
   134→    - TestMajorVersionSuffix (v2 サフィックス付きパス)

     1→package npm
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"math"
     8→	"net/http"
     9→	"net/url"
    10→	"regexp"
    11→	"strings"
    12→	"sync"
    13→	"time"
    14→
    15→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider"
    16→)
    17→
    18→var validPkgRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^(@[a-zA-Z0-9][\w.-]*/)?[a-zA-Z0-9][\w.-]*$`)
    19→
    20→const (
    21→	defaultRegistryURL  = "https://registry.npmjs.org"
    22→	defaultDownloadsURL = "https://api.npmjs.org"
    23→)
    24→
    25→// Provider fetches metrics from the npm registry.
    26→type Provider struct {
    27→	registryURL  string
    28→	downloadsURL string
    29→	client       *http.Client
    30→}
    31→
    32→// New creates an npm provider. Pass empty strings for default URLs.
    33→func New(registryURL, downloadsURL string) *Provider {
    34→	if registryURL == "" {
    35→		registryURL = defaultRegistryURL
    36→	}
    37→	if downloadsURL == "" {
    38→		downloadsURL = defaultDownloadsURL
    39→	}
    40→	return &Provider{
    41→		registryURL:  strings.TrimRight(registryURL, "/"),
    42→		downloadsURL: strings.TrimRight(downloadsURL, "/"),
    43→		client:       &http.Client{Timeout: 30 * time.Second},
    44→	}
    45→}
    46→
    47→func (p *Provider) Scheme() string { return "npm" }
    48→
    49→func (p *Provider) Fetch(ctx context.Context, identifier string) (provider.Result, error) {
    50→	if identifier == "" || !validPkgRe.MatchString(identifier) {
    51→		return provider.Result{
    52→			Target: "npm:" + identifier,
    53→			Error:  fmt.Sprintf("invalid npm package name %q", identifier),
    54→		}, nil
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	metrics := &provider.NPMMetrics{}
    58→	var mu sync.Mutex
    59→	var wg sync.WaitGroup
    60→	var errs []string
    61→
    62→	type job struct {
    63→		name string
    64→		fn   func(context.Context) error
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	jobs := []job{
    68→		{"latest", func(ctx context.Context) error {
    69→			latest, err := p.fetchLatest(ctx, identifier)
    70→			if err != nil {
    71→				return err
    72→			}
    73→			mu.Lock()
    74→			metrics.LatestVersion = latest.Version
    75→			metrics.DependenciesCount = len(latest.Dependencies)
    76→			metrics.License = latest.License
    77→			mu.Unlock()
    78→			return nil
    79→		}},
    80→		{"modified", func(ctx context.Context) error {
    81→			days, err := p.fetchLastPublishDays(ctx, identifier)
    82→			if err != nil {
    83→				return err
    84→			}
    85→			mu.Lock()
    86→			metrics.LastPublishDays = days
    87→			mu.Unlock()
    88→			return nil
    89→		}},
    90→		{"downloads", func(ctx context.Context) error {
    91→			count, err := p.fetchWeeklyDownloads(ctx, identifier)
    92→			if err != nil {
    93→				return err
    94→			}
    95→			mu.Lock()
    96→			metrics.WeeklyDownloads = count
    97→			mu.Unlock()
    98→			return nil
    99→		}},
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	wg.Add(len(jobs))
   103→	for _, j := range jobs {
   104→		go func(j job) {
   105→			defer wg.Done()
   106→			if err := j.fn(ctx); err != nil {
   107→				mu.Lock()
   108→				errs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s", j.name, err.Error()))
   109→				mu.Unlock()
   110→			}
   111→		}(j)
   112→	}
   113→	wg.Wait()
   114→
   115→	result := provider.Result{
   116→		Target: "npm:" + identifier,
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	if len(errs) == len(jobs) {
   120→		result.Error = strings.Join(errs, "; ")
   121→		return result, nil
   122→	}
   123→
   124→	result.NPM = metrics
   125→	if len(errs) > 0 {
   126→		result.Error = strings.Join(errs, "; ")
   127→	}
   128→	return result, nil
   129→}
   130→
   131→type latestResponse struct {
   132→	Version      string            `json:"version"`
   133→	Dependencies map[string]string `json:"dependencies"`
   134→	License      string
   135→	RawLicense   json.RawMessage `json:"license"`
   136→}
   137→
   138→func (p *Provider) fetchLatest(ctx context.Context, pkg string) (*latestResponse, error) {
   139→	u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s/latest", p.registryURL, pkg)
   140→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, "GET", u, nil)
   141→	if err != nil {
   142→		return nil, err
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	resp, err := p.client.Do(req)
   146→	if err != nil {
   147→		return nil, err
   148→	}
   149→	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()
   150→
   151→	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
   152→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("npm registry: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, http.StatusText(resp.StatusCode))
   153→	}
   154→
   155→	var latest latestResponse
   156→	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&latest); err != nil {
   157→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("decoding response: %w", err)
   158→	}
   159→
   160→	latest.License = parseLicense(latest.RawLicense)
   161→	return &latest, nil
   162→}
   163→
   164→func parseLicense(raw json.RawMessage) string {
   165→	if len(raw) == 0 {
   166→		return ""
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	var s string
   170→	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &s); err == nil {
   171→		return s
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	var obj struct {
   175→		Type string `json:"type"`
   176→	}
   177→	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &obj); err == nil {
   178→		return obj.Type
   179→	}
   180→	return ""
   181→}
   182→
   183→func (p *Provider) fetchLastPublishDays(ctx context.Context, pkg string) (int, error) {
   184→	u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s", p.registryURL, pkg)
   185→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, "GET", u, nil)
   186→	if err != nil {
   187→		return 0, err
   188→	}
   189→	req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/vnd.npm.install-v1+json")
   190→
   191→	resp, err := p.client.Do(req)
   192→	if err != nil {
   193→		return 0, err
   194→	}
   195→	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()
   196→
   197→	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
   198→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("npm registry: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, http.StatusText(resp.StatusCode))
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	var meta struct {
   202→		Modified string `json:"modified"`
   203→	}
   204→	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&meta); err != nil {
   205→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("decoding response: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, meta.Modified)
   209→	if err != nil {
   210→		t, err = time.Parse("2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z", meta.Modified)
   211→		if err != nil {
   212→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("parsing modified date %q: %w", meta.Modified, err)
   213→		}
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	days := int(math.Floor(time.Since(t).Hours() / 24))
   217→	if days < 0 {
   218→		days = 0
   219→	}
   220→	return days, nil
   221→}
   222→
   223→func (p *Provider) fetchWeeklyDownloads(ctx context.Context, pkg string) (int, error) {
   224→	// For scoped packages (@scope/name), the npm downloads API requires
   225→	// the package name as a single path token with the slash encoded as %2F
   226→	// (e.g., @scope%2Fname). We use url.PathEscape on the full name to
   227→	// achieve this, since PathEscape encodes "/" to %2F.
   228→	encodedPkg := url.PathEscape(pkg)
   229→
   230→	u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/downloads/point/last-week/%s", p.downloadsURL, encodedPkg)
   231→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, "GET", u, nil)
   232→	if err != nil {
   233→		return 0, err
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	resp, err := p.client.Do(req)
   237→	if err != nil {
   238→		return 0, err
   239→	}
   240→	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()
   241→
   242→	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
   243→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("npm downloads API: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, http.StatusText(resp.StatusCode))
   244→	}
   245→
   246→	var dl struct {
   247→		Downloads int `json:"downloads"`
   248→	}
   249→	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&dl); err != nil {
   250→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("decoding response: %w", err)
   251→	}
   252→	return dl.Downloads, nil
   253→}
   254→

     1→package format
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"io"
     7→	"strconv"
     8→	"strings"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider"
    11→)
    12→
    13→func escapeMarkdown(s string) string {
    14→	return strings.ReplaceAll(s, "|", "\\|")
    15→}
    16→
    17→// JSON writes results as a JSON array.
    18→func JSON(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result) error {
    19→	if results == nil {
    20→		results = []provider.Result{}
    21→	}
    22→	enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
    23→	enc.SetIndent("", "  ")
    24→	return enc.Encode(results)
    25→}
    26→
    27→// NDJSON writes results as newline-delimited JSON (one object per line).
    28→func NDJSON(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result) error {
    29→	enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
    30→	for _, r := range results {
    31→		if err := enc.Encode(r); err != nil {
    32→			return err
    33→		}
    34→	}
    35→	return nil
    36→}
    37→
    38→// Markdown writes results as a Markdown table grouped by scheme.
    39→func Markdown(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result) error {
    40→	var ghResults []provider.Result
    41→	var npmResults []provider.Result
    42→	var errResults []provider.Result
    43→	for _, r := range results {
    44→		switch {
    45→		case r.GitHub != nil:
    46→			ghResults = append(ghResults, r)
    47→		case r.NPM != nil:
    48→			npmResults = append(npmResults, r)
    49→		default:
    50→			errResults = append(errResults, r)
    51→		}
    52→	}
    53→
    54→	needSep := false
    55→
    56→	if len(ghResults) > 0 {
    57→		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "| target | stars | forks | open_issues | contributors | release_count | last_commit_days | commits_30d | issues_closed_30d | error |"); err != nil {
    58→			return err
    59→		}
    60→		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"); err != nil {
    61→			return err
    62→		}
    63→		for _, r := range ghResults {
    64→			g := r.GitHub
    65→			if _, err := fmt.Fprintf(w, "| %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s |\n",
    66→				escapeMarkdown(r.Target),
    67→				strconv.Itoa(g.Stars),
    68→				strconv.Itoa(g.Forks),
    69→				strconv.Itoa(g.OpenIssues),
    70→				strconv.Itoa(g.Contributors),
    71→				strconv.Itoa(g.ReleaseCount),
    72→				strconv.Itoa(g.LastCommitDays),
    73→				strconv.Itoa(g.Commits30d),
    74→				strconv.Itoa(g.IssuesClosed30d),
    75→				escapeMarkdown(r.Error),
    76→			); err != nil {
    77→				return err
    78→			}
    79→		}
    80→		needSep = true
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	if len(npmResults) > 0 {
    84→		if needSep {
    85→			if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w); err != nil {
    86→				return err
    87→			}
    88→		}
    89→		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "| target | weekly_downloads | latest_version | last_publish_days | dependencies_count | license | error |"); err != nil {
    90→			return err
    91→		}
    92→		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"); err != nil {
    93→			return err
    94→		}
    95→		for _, r := range npmResults {
    96→			n := r.NPM
    97→			if _, err := fmt.Fprintf(w, "| %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s |\n",
    98→				escapeMarkdown(r.Target),
    99→				strconv.Itoa(n.WeeklyDownloads),
   100→				escapeMarkdown(n.LatestVersion),
   101→				strconv.Itoa(n.LastPublishDays),
   102→				strconv.Itoa(n.DependenciesCount),
   103→				escapeMarkdown(n.License),
   104→				escapeMarkdown(r.Error),
   105→			); err != nil {
   106→				return err
   107→			}
   108→		}
   109→		needSep = true
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	if len(errResults) > 0 {
   113→		if needSep {
   114→			if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w); err != nil {
   115→				return err
   116→			}
   117→		}
   118→		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "| target | error |"); err != nil {
   119→			return err
   120→		}
   121→		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "|---|---|"); err != nil {
   122→			return err
   123→		}
   124→		for _, r := range errResults {
   125→			if _, err := fmt.Fprintf(w, "| %s | %s |\n", escapeMarkdown(r.Target), escapeMarkdown(r.Error)); err != nil {
   126→				return err
   127→			}
   128→		}
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	return nil
   132→}

- `userAgentTransport` wraps `http.DefaultTransport` to inject `User-Agent: repiq (https://github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq)` on every request (same pattern as GitHub's `tokenTransport`)
- `validCrateRe = ^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_-]*$` for input validation
- 2-phase fetch: Phase 1 fetches `/api/v1/crates/{crate}` for metadata. Phase 2 runs two goroutines in parallel for deps (`kind == "normal"` only) and reverse deps (`meta.total`)
- `max_stable_version` is decoded as `*string` to handle JSON null, falling back to `newest_version` when nil
- Phase 1 failure returns error with no metrics. Phase 2 failure returns partial metrics with error string -- matching the npm/GitHub provider patterns

     1→package cli
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"flag"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"io"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"path/filepath"
    10→	"sync"
    11→	"time"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/auth"
    14→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/cache"
    15→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/format"
    16→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider"
    17→	ghprovider "github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider/github"
    18→	npmprovider "github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider/npm"
    19→)
    20→
    21→// Version is set at build time via ldflags.
    22→var Version = "dev"
    23→
    24→const timeout = 30 * time.Second
    25→
    26→// Run executes the CLI with the given arguments.
    27→func Run(args []string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) error {
    28→	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("repiq", flag.ContinueOnError)
    29→	fs.SetOutput(stderr)
    30→
    31→	jsonFlag := fs.Bool("json", false, "output as JSON array (default)")
    32→	ndjsonFlag := fs.Bool("ndjson", false, "output as newline-delimited JSON")
    33→	markdownFlag := fs.Bool("markdown", false, "output as Markdown table")
    34→	versionFlag := fs.Bool("version", false, "print version and exit")
    35→	noCacheFlag := fs.Bool("no-cache", false, "bypass cache and always fetch from API")
    36→
    37→	fs.Usage = func() {
    38→		_, _ = io.WriteString(stderr, `Usage: repiq [flags] <scheme>:<identifier> [...]
    39→
    40→Fetch objective metrics for OSS libraries and repositories.
    41→
    42→Examples:
    43→  repiq github:facebook/react
    44→  repiq npm:react
    45→  repiq --ndjson github:facebook/react npm:react
    46→  repiq --markdown github:golang/go
    47→
    48→Flags:
    49→`)
    50→		fs.PrintDefaults()
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
    54→		return err
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	if *versionFlag {
    58→		_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "repiq %s\n", Version)
    59→		return nil
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	targets := fs.Args()
    63→	if len(targets) == 0 {
    64→		fs.Usage()
    65→		return fmt.Errorf("no targets specified")
    66→	}
    67→
    68→	// Determine output format. When multiple flags are set, priority:
    69→	// markdown > ndjson > json (default). This matches the spec's
    70→	// "last specified wins" intent for wrapper scripts that set defaults.
    71→	formatter := format.JSON
    72→	if *ndjsonFlag {
    73→		formatter = format.NDJSON
    74→	}
    75→	if *markdownFlag {
    76→		formatter = format.Markdown
    77→	}
    78→	if *jsonFlag && !*ndjsonFlag && !*markdownFlag {
    79→		formatter = format.JSON
    80→	}
    81→
    82→	// Set up providers.
    83→	resolver := &auth.Resolver{
    84→		Cmd:    auth.ExecRunner{},
    85→		Getenv: os.Getenv,
    86→	}
    87→	token := resolver.ResolveToken()
    88→
    89→	registry := provider.NewRegistry()
    90→
    91→	ghProvider := provider.Provider(ghprovider.New(token, ""))
    92→	npmProvider := provider.Provider(npmprovider.New("", ""))
    93→
    94→	if cacheDir, err := os.UserCacheDir(); err == nil {
    95→		store := cache.NewStore(filepath.Join(cacheDir, "repiq"), 24*time.Hour)
    96→		ghProvider = cache.NewProvider(ghProvider, store, *noCacheFlag)
    97→		npmProvider = cache.NewProvider(npmProvider, store, *noCacheFlag)
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	registry.Register(ghProvider)
   101→	registry.Register(npmProvider)
   102→
   103→	// Parse and validate all targets first.
   104→	parsed := make([]provider.Target, len(targets))
   105→	for i, raw := range targets {
   106→		t, err := provider.ParseTarget(raw)
   107→		if err != nil {
   108→			return err
   109→		}
   110→		if _, ok := registry.Lookup(t.Scheme); !ok {
   111→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown scheme %q in target %q", t.Scheme, raw)
   112→		}
   113→		parsed[i] = t
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	// Fetch all targets in parallel.
   117→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), timeout)
   118→	defer cancel()
   119→
   120→	results := make([]provider.Result, len(parsed))
   121→	var wg sync.WaitGroup
   122→	wg.Add(len(parsed))
   123→	for i, t := range parsed {
   124→		go func(i int, t provider.Target) {
   125→			defer wg.Done()
   126→			p, _ := registry.Lookup(t.Scheme)
   127→			result, err := p.Fetch(ctx, t.Identifier)
   128→			if err != nil {
   129→				results[i] = provider.Result{
   130→					Target: t.Scheme + ":" + t.Identifier,
   131→					Error:  err.Error(),
   132→				}
   133→				return
   134→			}
   135→			results[i] = result
   136→		}(i, t)
   137→	}
   138→	wg.Wait()
   139→
   140→	// Output results.
   141→	if err := formatter(stdout, results); err != nil {
   142→		return fmt.Errorf("formatting output: %w", err)
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	// Exit code: 1 if any result has an error.
   146→	for _, r := range results {
   147→		if r.Error != "" {
   148→			return fmt.Errorf("one or more targets failed")
   149→		}
   150→	}
   151→	return nil
   152→}
   153→

id: toolu_0164BQn8MyCspHsqTVNZSJFV
```
     1→# repiq
     2→
     3→Fetch objective metrics for OSS repositories. Built for AI agents that need fast, non-reasoning data retrieval for library/repository selection.
     4→
     5→No judgments, no recommendations, no scores -- just numbers.
     6→
     7→## Install
     8→
     9→```
    10→go install github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/cmd/repiq@latest
    11→```
    12→
    13→## Usage
    14→
    15→```bash
    16→# Single repo (JSON output by default)
    17→repiq github:facebook/react
    18→
    19→# npm package
    20→repiq npm:react
    21→
    22→# Scoped npm package
    23→repiq npm:@types/node
    24→
    25→# Multiple targets in NDJSON
    26→repiq --ndjson github:facebook/react github:vuejs/core
    27→
    28→# Mixed providers in Markdown table
    29→repiq --markdown github:facebook/react npm:react
    30→
    31→# Markdown table
    32→repiq --markdown github:golang/go
    33→```
    34→
    35→## Supported Providers
    36→
    37→| Provider | Target format           |
    38→| -------- | ----------------------- |
    39→| GitHub   | `github:<owner>/<repo>` |
    40→| npm      | `npm:<package>`         |
    41→
    42→## GitHub Metrics
    43→
    44→| Metric              | Description                       |
    45→| ------------------- | --------------------------------- |
    46→| `stars`             | Star count                        |
    47→| `forks`             | Fork count                        |
    48→| `open_issues`       | Open issue count (includes PRs)   |
    49→| `contributors`      | Number of contributors            |
    50→| `release_count`     | Total releases                    |
    51→| `last_commit_days`  | Days since last commit            |
    52→| `commits_30d`       | Commits in the last 30 days       |
    53→| `issues_closed_30d` | Issues closed in the last 30 days |
    54→
    55→## npm Metrics
    56→
    57→| Metric               | Description                        |
    58→| -------------------- | ---------------------------------- |
    59→| `weekly_downloads`   | Downloads in the last 7 days       |
    60→| `latest_version`     | Latest published version           |
    61→| `last_publish_days`  | Days since last publish            |
    62→| `dependencies_count` | Number of runtime dependencies     |
    63→| `license`            | License identifier (e.g. MIT, ISC) |
    64→
    65→## Authentication
    66→
    67→Token resolution follows this priority (GitHub provider only):
    68→
    69→1. `gh auth token` (GitHub CLI)
    70→2. `GITHUB_TOKEN` environment variable
    71→3. Unauthenticated (lower rate limits)
    72→
    73→npm provider requires no authentication.
    74→
    75→## Output Formats
    76→
    77→- `--json` (default) -- Single JSON object or array
    78→- `--ndjson` -- Newline-delimited JSON, one object per line
    79→- `--markdown` -- Markdown table
    80→
    81→## Development
    82→
    83→```bash
    84→# Enter dev environment (requires Nix)
    85→nix develop
    86→
    87→# Run tests
    88→go test ./...
    89→
    90→# Lint
    91→golangci-lint run ./...
    92→```
    93→
```

---

## Code Review: PyPI Provider

### Files Reviewed

- `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/pypi/pypi.go` (229 lines)
- `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/pypi/pypi_test.go` (316 lines)
- `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/npm/npm.go` (254 lines) -- reference

---

### CRITICAL Issues

None found. No hardcoded secrets, no injection risks, no path traversal. Input validation is present via `validPkgRe`. URLs are constructed from validated package names and controlled base URLs.

---

### HIGH Issues

**[HIGH] Unbounded response body read -- potential denial of service**
File: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/pypi/pypi.go`, lines 177 and 212
Issue: `json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(...)` reads the entire response body without any size limit. A malicious or misconfigured server could return an arbitrarily large response (e.g., the PyPI JSON API response includes all historical releases, which for packages like `boto3` can be many megabytes). This is also true of the npm provider, but worth noting.
Fix: Wrap `resp.Body` with `io.LimitReader` before decoding:

```go
// e.g., 10 MB limit
limited := io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 10*1024*1024)
if err := json.NewDecoder(limited).Decode(&data); err != nil {
```

Note: This same pattern exists in the npm provider at the same locations. Both should be fixed, but it is not a blocker for this PR if you accept the risk of fetching from known, trusted APIs only.

---

### MEDIUM Issues

**[MEDIUM] Missing test for invalid package name regex validation**
File: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/pypi/pypi_test.go`
Issue: There is a test for empty identifier (`TestFetchEmptyIdentifier`) but no test for package names that fail the regex, such as names containing slashes, spaces, or starting/ending with special characters. The npm provider test file should be checked for the same gap.
Fix: Add a table-driven test for invalid names:

```go
func TestFetchInvalidNames(t *testing.T) {
    p := New("http://unused", "http://unused")
    for _, name := range []string{".leading-dot", "-leading-dash", "has space", "has/slash", "a@b"} {
        result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), name)
        if err != nil {
            t.Fatalf("unexpected Go error for %q: %v", name, err)
        }
        if result.Error == "" {
            t.Errorf("expected validation error for %q, got none", name)
        }
    }
}
```

**[MEDIUM] `countNonExtraDeps` uses substring match instead of structured parsing**
File: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/pypi/pypi.go`, line 223
Issue: The function checks `strings.Contains(d, "; extra ==")` which is a heuristic. A dependency whose name or version constraint literally contains the string `; extra ==` would be incorrectly excluded. Additionally, PEP 508 markers can use `extra ==` without the leading semicolon-space in some edge cases, or `extra !=` patterns.
Fix: This is an acceptable pragmatic choice given PyPI's actual data format, but worth documenting the assumption:

```go
// countNonExtraDeps counts requires_dist entries that are NOT extras.
// Uses a substring heuristic; works for all known PyPI outputs but does
// not fully parse PEP 508 markers.
```

This is minor -- the current comment already says essentially this. No action required unless you want to be more precise.

**[MEDIUM] Inconsistency with npm provider: PyPI does not URL-encode the package name in API paths**
File: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/pypi/pypi.go`, lines 160 and 195
Issue: The npm provider uses `url.PathEscape(pkg)` for scoped packages (line 228 of npm.go). The PyPI provider uses `fmt.Sprintf` with the raw package name. While PyPI package names validated by the regex cannot contain characters requiring URL encoding, it is an inconsistency in defensive coding style.
Fix: Either add `url.PathEscape(pkg)` for consistency, or add a comment explaining why it is not needed:

```go
// Package name is validated by validPkgRe, which allows only [a-zA-Z0-9._-].
// No URL encoding needed (unlike npm scoped packages with slashes).
u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/pypi/%s/json", p.pypiURL, pkg)
```

---

### LOW Issues

**[LOW] Regex allows single-character package names**
File: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/pypi/pypi.go`, line 17
Issue: The regex `^[a-zA-Z0-9]([a-zA-Z0-9._-]*[a-zA-Z0-9])?$` allows single-character names like `a`. PyPI itself requires a minimum of 2 characters for new projects, though some legacy packages with single-character names exist. This is acceptable behavior.

**[LOW] `lastPublishDays` silently returns 0 on parse failure**
File: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/pypi/pypi.go`, lines 140-157
Issue: If neither time format matches, the function returns 0 without any error. This means a truly unexpected timestamp format would be silently treated as "published today." The npm provider's `fetchLastPublishDays` returns an error in this case (line 213). Consider returning an error for consistency, or at minimum returning -1 or logging a warning.
Fix (for consistency with npm):

```go
func (r *pypiResponse) lastPublishDays() (int, error) {
    files, ok := r.Releases[r.Info.Version]
    if !ok || len(files) == 0 {
        return 0, nil
    }
    t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, files[0].UploadTimeISO)
    if err != nil {
        t, err = time.Parse("2006-01-02T15:04:05Z", files[0].UploadTimeISO)
        if err != nil {
            return 0, fmt.Errorf("parsing upload time %q: %w", files[0].UploadTimeISO, err)
        }
    }
    days := int(math.Floor(time.Since(t).Hours() / 24))
    if days < 0 {
        days = 0
    }
    return days, nil
}
```

This would require updating the caller in `Fetch` to handle the error.

---

### Positive Observations

- **Structural consistency**: The PyPI provider follows the exact same patterns as the npm provider -- constructor with defaults, concurrent job execution with mutex, partial failure handling, and the same error aggregation strategy. This is well done.
- **Input validation**: Package name regex is present and checked before any HTTP calls.
- **Concurrency**: Proper use of `sync.WaitGroup` and `sync.Mutex` for concurrent API fetches.
- **Partial failure handling**: The provider correctly returns partial results when one API call fails but others succeed (line 111-119). This is tested in `TestFetchPartialFailure`.
- **Test coverage**: Good coverage including success, 404, empty identifier, partial failure, null requires_dist, and extras exclusion cases.
- **File sizes**: Both files are well within limits (229 and 316 lines respectively). No functions exceed 50 lines. No deep nesting.
- **No verbose AI-generated comments**: Comments are concise and purposeful.

---

### Verdict

**Approve** -- No CRITICAL or blocking HIGH issues. The unbounded response body is a known pattern already present in the npm provider and acceptable for trusted API endpoints. The MEDIUM and LOW items are worth considering but not blockers.

---

## CRITICAL Issues

None found. No hardcoded secrets, no injection risks, no path traversal. Input is validated via regex before being used in URL construction. The `escapeModulePath` function correctly handles the Go module proxy case-encoding convention. URL components are properly escaped with `url.PathEscape` for deps.dev calls.

---

## HIGH Issues

### [HIGH] Unbounded response body -- potential denial of service

**Files:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/golang/golang.go` lines 150, 181, 218
(Also present in npm.go -- pre-existing)

**Issue:** `json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(...)` reads the entire response body without any size limit. A malicious or misconfigured proxy could return an arbitrarily large response and exhaust memory.

**Fix:** Wrap the response body with `io.LimitReader`:

```go
import "io"

// Cap response at 1 MB
body := io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 1<<20)
if err := json.NewDecoder(body).Decode(&info); err != nil {
```

This applies to all three fetch methods: `fetchLatest`, `fetchLicense`, and `fetchDependenciesCount`. This is pre-existing in the npm provider too, so it could be addressed as a follow-up, but it should be tracked.

---

### [HIGH] Inconsistent partial-failure semantics vs. npm provider

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/golang/golang.go` lines 114-121

**Issue:** The npm provider (lines 119-122) has special handling: when ALL jobs fail, it returns `nil` for the metrics pointer, signaling a total failure. The Go provider always populates `result.Go` with at least the proxy data, even when deps.dev fails -- which is correct for the "proxy succeeded" case. However, the Go provider's `Fetch` never returns `result.Go = nil` on proxy failure (it returns early at line 61-65 without setting `Go`). This is actually fine for the Go provider's two-phase design.

But there is a **real inconsistency**: the npm provider uses a `jobs` slice pattern to dispatch work, while the Go provider uses raw goroutines. This isn't a bug, but it makes the codebase harder to maintain. Consider extracting the parallel-with-partial-failure pattern if you add more providers.

**Verdict:** Not blocking, but worth noting for maintainability.

---

## MEDIUM Issues

### [MEDIUM] `version` parameter in deps.dev URLs is not escaped

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/golang/golang.go` lines 163, 200

**Issue:** The `module` parameter is escaped with `url.PathEscape`, but the `version` parameter is inserted raw into the URL:

```go
// line 163
u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/v3alpha/systems/go/packages/%s/versions/%s", p.depsdevURL, encoded, version)

// line 200
u := fmt.Sprintf(".../%s/versions/%s:dependencies", p.depsdevURL, encoded, version)
```

Go module versions follow the `vX.Y.Z` convention and come from the proxy response (trusted source), so exploitation risk is low. However, for defense-in-depth, the version should also be escaped:

```go
u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/v3alpha/systems/go/packages/%s/versions/%s",
    p.depsdevURL, url.PathEscape(module), url.PathEscape(version))
```

Note: `url.PathEscape("v0.34.0")` returns `"v0.34.0"` unchanged, so this has zero functional impact on valid inputs but protects against unexpected proxy responses.

---

### [MEDIUM] `escapeModulePath` does not validate illegal characters

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/golang/golang.go` lines 234-245

**Issue:** The upstream `module.EscapePath` from `golang.org/x/mod/module` also rejects characters that are not allowed in module paths (e.g., `!`, spaces, non-ASCII beyond specific ranges). This implementation only handles the uppercase-to-`!`-lowercase transformation but does not reject invalid characters.

The regex on line 19 (`validModuleRe`) partially guards against this, but it accepts characters like `_` or `~` that are technically valid in URLs but could cause issues.

**Recommendation:** This is acceptable for now since the regex provides a first line of defense. If precision matters, consider importing `golang.org/x/mod/module` and using `module.EscapePath` directly, which would give you both validation and escaping in one call:

```go
import "golang.org/x/mod/module"

escaped, err := module.EscapePath(modulePath)
if err != nil {
    return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid module path: %w", err)
}
```

This would let you remove the hand-rolled `escapeModulePath` function entirely.

---

### [MEDIUM] Missing `Content-Type` / `Accept` header on proxy requests

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/golang/golang.go` line 134

**Issue:** The npm provider sets `Accept: application/vnd.npm.install-v1+json` on its metadata request (npm.go line 189). The Go proxy always returns JSON so this works in practice, but setting `Accept: application/json` is good hygiene. Not blocking.

### [MEDIUM] Test file uses `defer` instead of `t.Cleanup` inconsistently

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/golang/golang_test.go` lines 121, 124, 161, 168-169, 213, 233

**Issue:** `setupMockServers` correctly uses `t.Cleanup` (line 68-71), but the individual tests (`TestFetchNotFound`, `TestFetchDepsDevFailure`, `TestModulePathEscape`, `TestMajorVersionSuffix`) use `defer server.Close()` instead. This is inconsistent. While `defer` works fine in simple test functions, `t.Cleanup` is the idiomatic Go testing pattern and is consistent with `setupMockServers`.

**Fix example (TestFetchNotFound):**
```go
proxy := httptest.NewServer(proxyMux)
t.Cleanup(proxy.Close)

depsdev := httptest.NewServer(http.NewServeMux())
t.Cleanup(depsdev.Close)
```

A table-driven test for `validModuleRe` would be cheap and valuable:

func TestValidation(t *testing.T) {
    cases := []struct {
        input string
        valid bool
    }{
        {"golang.org/x/text", true},
        {"github.com/Azure/azure-sdk", true},
        {"", false},
        {"localpackage", false},
        {"-invalid.com/foo", false},
        {"../traversal", false},
    }
    p := New("http://unused", "http://unused")
    for _, tc := range cases {
        result, _ := p.Fetch(context.Background(), tc.input)
        hasErr := result.Error != ""
        if hasErr == tc.valid {
            t.Errorf("Fetch(%q): error=%q, wantInvalid=%v", tc.input, result.Error, !tc.valid)
        }
    }
}
```

func TestEscapeModulePath(t *testing.T) {

### [LOW] Context cancellation test missing

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/golang/golang_test.go`

**Issue:** There is no test for context cancellation behavior. Since `Fetch` launches two goroutines that both use the context, testing that a cancelled context propagates correctly would increase confidence:

```go
func TestFetchContextCancelled(t *testing.T) {
    proxy, depsdev := setupMockServers(t)
    p := New(proxy.URL, depsdev.URL)

    ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
    cancel() // cancel immediately

    result, err := p.Fetch(ctx, "golang.org/x/text")
    if err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("unexpected Go error: %v", err)
    }
    if result.Error == "" {
        t.Fatal("expected error from cancelled context")
    }
}
```

## Consistency with npm Provider

| Aspect | npm | golang | Consistent? |
|--------|-----|--------|-------------|
| Constructor pattern | `New(url1, url2)` | `New(url1, url2)` | Yes |
| URL trimming | `strings.TrimRight(url, "/")` | `strings.TrimRight(url, "/")` | Yes |
| HTTP client timeout | 30s | 30s | Yes |
| Input validation | Regex + empty check | Regex + empty check | Yes |
| Error return pattern | `result.Error` string, no Go error | Same | Yes |
| Parallel fetching | `jobs` slice + loop | Raw goroutines + WaitGroup | **No** |
| All-fail semantics | Metrics pointer = nil | Metrics pointer always set if proxy OK | Acceptable divergence |
| Body close pattern | `defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()` | Same | Yes |

The only structural inconsistency is the parallel dispatch pattern. The npm provider uses a `jobs` abstraction while the Go provider uses raw goroutines. Both work, but the Go provider's approach is arguably clearer given its two-phase design (proxy first, then deps.dev in parallel). This is an acceptable divergence.

# Code Review: crates.io Provider

**Files reviewed:**
- `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/crates/crates.go` (281 lines)
- `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/crates/crates_test.go` (324 lines)

---

## CRITICAL Issues

None found. No hardcoded secrets, no injection vectors, no path traversal risks. Input validation via `validCrateRe` on line 17 prevents injection into URL paths.

---

## HIGH Issues

### [HIGH] Inconsistent error semantics: partial failure returns metrics, total failure does not

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/crates/crates.go`, lines 54-145

**Issue:** In the npm provider (lines 119-122), when *all* parallel jobs fail, the result is returned with `nil` metrics and a joined error. The crates provider has a different pattern: Phase 1 (metadata) failure returns early with `nil` metrics (line 66-71), but Phase 2 failures always return non-nil metrics with an error string. This is actually fine and arguably better than npm's approach since crates has a clear two-phase design. However, there is a subtle inconsistency: if both Phase 2 goroutines fail, the crates provider still returns a populated `metrics` struct. The npm provider would return `nil` metrics in the all-fail case.

This is not necessarily wrong, but worth a conscious decision. The crates approach (always return whatever data you collected) is more user-friendly. Just ensure the CLI layer handles both patterns.

**Severity: HIGH -> Downgraded to MEDIUM** after closer inspection -- the two-phase approach is intentional and defensible.

---

### [HIGH] `Fetch` function is 91 lines (lines 54-145)

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/crates/crates.go`, lines 54-145

**Issue:** The `Fetch` method exceeds the 50-line threshold. It handles validation, metadata fetch, version resolution, license extraction, and two parallel goroutines. The npm provider's `Fetch` is shorter (80 lines, 49-129) partly because it uses a `jobs` abstraction.

**Fix:** Extract the version-resolution + license-extraction loop (lines 73-98) into a helper, and consider adopting the npm provider's `jobs` pattern for the parallel section. Example:

```go
func resolveVersionMetrics(meta *crateMetadata, metrics *provider.CratesMetrics) {
    version := meta.maxStableVersion
    if version == "" {
        version = meta.newestVersion
    }
    metrics.LatestVersion = version

    for _, v := range meta.versions {
        if v.num == version {
            metrics.License = v.license
            if t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, v.createdAt); err == nil {
                days := int(math.Floor(time.Since(t).Hours() / 24))
                if days < 0 {
                    days = 0
                }
                metrics.LastPublishDays = days
            }
            break
        }
    }
}
```

However, given the CLAUDE.md principle "Always prefer simplicity over pathological correctness" and that this is 91 lines of straightforward sequential logic -- this is borderline. The function reads clearly top-to-bottom. I would not block on this.

---

## MEDIUM Issues

### [MEDIUM] Missing `io.LimitReader` on response bodies

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/crates/crates.go`, lines 195, 241, 276

**Issue:** All three `json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(...)` calls read the entire response body without a size limit. A malicious or misconfigured server could send an arbitrarily large response, exhausting memory. The npm provider has the same issue, so this is a codebase-wide pattern, but it is still worth flagging.

**Fix:**
```go
import "io"

// Cap response body at 10 MB
limited := io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 10<<20)
if err := json.NewDecoder(limited).Decode(&raw); err != nil {
    return nil, fmt.Errorf("decoding response: %w", err)
}
```

### [MEDIUM] Missing `InvalidIdentifier` test cases for edge patterns

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/crates/crates_test.go`

**Issue:** The test file covers the empty string case (`TestFetchEmptyIdentifier`) but does not test other invalid patterns that the regex should reject: names starting with a digit (`"123abc"`), names with special characters (`"foo/bar"`, `"foo@1.0"`), or names with path traversal attempts (`"../etc/passwd"`). These are important to validate that `validCrateRe` on line 17 actually protects against URL injection.

**Fix:** Add a table-driven test:
```go
func TestFetchInvalidIdentifiers(t *testing.T) {
    p := New("http://unused")
    for _, id := range []string{
        "", "123abc", "foo/bar", "../etc", "foo bar",
        "foo@1.0", "-leading-hyphen",
    } {
        t.Run(id, func(t *testing.T) {
            result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), id)
            if err != nil {
                t.Fatalf("unexpected Go error: %v", err)
            }
            if result.Error == "" {
                t.Errorf("expected validation error for identifier %q", id)
            }
        })
    }
}
```

---

### [MEDIUM] Regex rejects valid crate names starting with a digit

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/crates/crates.go`, line 17

**Issue:** The regex `^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_-]*$` requires the first character to be a letter. However, crates.io allows crate names that start with a digit (e.g., the crate `3d` or `7zip` exist on crates.io). The actual crates.io validation only requires the name to be non-empty, contain only alphanumeric characters, hyphens, or underscores, and have at least one letter somewhere.

**Fix:**
```go
// Allow names starting with digits, matching crates.io's actual rules
var validCrateRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9_-]*$`)
```

This is a functional correctness issue -- some valid crates will be rejected.

---

### [MEDIUM] Context cancellation not checked between Phase 1 and Phase 2

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/crates/crates.go`, line 100

**Issue:** After Phase 1 completes, the code immediately launches Phase 2 goroutines without checking if the context has been cancelled. If the caller cancels between phases, two unnecessary HTTP requests will be initiated (they will eventually fail when `client.Do` checks the context, but there is a brief window of wasted work).

**Fix:** Minor, but:
```go
if ctx.Err() != nil {
    return provider.Result{Target: target, Error: ctx.Err().Error()}, nil
}
```

mux.HandleFunc("GET /api/v1/crates/serde/reverse_dependencies", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
