### [LOW] `version` parameter in `fetchDependenciesCount` is not validated

**Issue:** The `version` string is interpolated into the URL without validation. In practice, this is safe because the version comes from the API response (not user input), but as a defense-in-depth measure, `url.PathEscape(version)` would be safer, especially since semver pre-release versions can contain `+` characters (e.g., `1.0.0+build.1`).

**Fix:**
```go
u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/api/v1/crates/%s/%s/dependencies", p.baseURL, crate, url.PathEscape(version))
```

## Positive Observations

1. **Input validation** (line 17, 57): The regex check before any HTTP call is the right pattern. This prevents URL injection.

2. **User-Agent header** (lines 22-30): crates.io requires a User-Agent per their policy. This is correctly implemented and tested.

3. **Graceful partial failure** (lines 100-144): Phase 2 failures do not discard Phase 1 data. This is user-friendly.

4. **Pre-release fallback** (lines 73-76): Handling `max_stable_version: null` by falling back to `newest_version` is correct and tested.

6. **File sizes**: crates.go is 281 lines, crates_test.go is 324 lines. Both well within limits.

7. **No hardcoded secrets, no console logging, no TODO/FIXME comments**.

8. **Consistent patterns**: The code follows the same structural patterns as the npm provider (constructor with base URL, `Scheme()` method, parallel fetching, error collection via mutex).

**Approve** -- No CRITICAL or blocking HIGH issues.

The code is clean, well-structured, and follows established patterns from the npm provider. The two actionable items I would most recommend addressing before merge are:

2. **[MEDIUM] Add invalid-identifier test cases** -- strengthen confidence in the validation layer.

Everything else is minor hardening that can be addressed later.

# Code Review: Registry Providers + Format + CLI Changes

## Summary

This PR adds PyPI, crates.io, and Go Modules providers (3 new registries), extends the Markdown formatter to support all 5 provider types, introduces a provider registry pattern, and updates the CLI to wire everything together. The diff is +2,123 / -19 lines across 11 files.

Overall, this is well-structured code with good test coverage, proper input validation, context propagation, and graceful partial-failure handling. There are no critical or high-severity issues. Below are findings organized by priority.

---

## CRITICAL Issues

None found. No hardcoded secrets, no injection risks, no authentication bypasses.

---

## HIGH Issues

None found. Error handling is thorough, functions are reasonably sized, and no file exceeds 400 lines.

---

## MEDIUM Issues

### [MEDIUM-1] URL path injection in PyPI provider -- identifier is not URL-escaped

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/pypi/pypi.go:160`

The `pkg` parameter is validated by regex, but it is interpolated directly into the URL path without `url.PathEscape`. The regex `^[a-zA-Z0-9]([a-zA-Z0-9._-]*[a-zA-Z0-9])?$` does allow `.` and `-`, which are safe in URL paths, so this is not exploitable in practice. However, the Go module provider correctly uses `url.PathEscape` (line 162 of `golang.go`), creating an inconsistency. The crates provider has the same pattern (line 166 of `crates.go`).

This is defense-in-depth -- the regex guards it, but consistency matters.

```go
// pypi.go:160 -- current
u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/pypi/%s/json", p.pypiURL, pkg)

// suggested -- consistent with golang.go
u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/pypi/%s/json", p.pypiURL, url.PathEscape(pkg))
```

Same applies to `crates.go:166`, `crates.go:218`, `crates.go:255`.

### [MEDIUM-2] Markdown function is 192 lines with heavy repetition

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/format/format.go:39-231`

The `Markdown` function is nearly 200 lines and each provider section follows an identical pattern: check length, optionally write separator, write header, write separator row, iterate and write data rows. This is a strong candidate for extracting a helper.

```go
// Suggested refactor sketch:
type tableConfig struct {
    header  []string
    rowFunc func(r provider.Result) []string
}

func writeTable(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result, cfg tableConfig) error {
    // write header, separator, and rows using cfg
}
```

This would reduce `Markdown` from ~200 lines to ~50 lines and make adding new providers trivial.

### [MEDIUM-3] `needSep` on line 208 is set but never read (dead code)

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/format/format.go:208`

```go
needSep = true  // line 208, after goResults block
```

This assignment on line 208 is the last section before `errResults`. The `errResults` block on line 211 already checks `needSep`, so line 208 is not dead code per se -- it is used. However, if Go results is the last block *and* there are no error results, this is a harmless write. This is fine. (Retracted -- this is correct behavior.)

### [MEDIUM-4] Silently ignoring cache initialization failure

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/cli/cli.go:103-110`

```go
if cacheDir, err := os.UserCacheDir(); err == nil {
    // ...wrap providers in cache...
}
```

If `UserCacheDir()` fails, the CLI silently proceeds without caching. This is likely intentional (graceful degradation), but consider logging a debug-level warning to stderr so users can diagnose why cache is not working.

### [MEDIUM-5] JSON response body size is unbounded

**Files:**
- `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/crates/crates.go:195`
- `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/pypi/pypi.go:177`
- `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/golang/golang.go:150`

All providers decode JSON directly from `resp.Body` without limiting the response size. A malicious or misconfigured server could send an arbitrarily large response, causing OOM. Consider wrapping with `io.LimitReader`:

```go
// Before decoding:
limited := io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 10<<20) // 10 MB max
if err := json.NewDecoder(limited).Decode(&data); err != nil {
```

This is low-risk for well-known public APIs but worth noting for defense-in-depth, especially since `baseURL` is configurable.

### [MEDIUM-6] Duplicate timeout: both CLI and each provider set 30s

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/cli/cli.go:27` and each provider's `New()` function

The CLI sets a 30-second context timeout (line 132), and each HTTP client also has a 30-second timeout. These are independent -- the context cancellation and HTTP client timeout race each other. The HTTP client timeout is redundant when the context is always passed. Consider removing the per-client timeout or making it slightly longer than the CLI timeout to avoid confusing timeout error messages.

---

## LOW Issues

### [LOW-1] Format flag priority logic is slightly confusing

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/cli/cli.go:77-86`

```go
formatter := format.JSON           // default
if *ndjsonFlag {
    formatter = format.NDJSON
}
if *markdownFlag {
    formatter = format.Markdown
}
if *jsonFlag && !*ndjsonFlag && !*markdownFlag {
    formatter = format.JSON
}
```

The last condition (lines 84-86) is a no-op -- `formatter` is already `format.JSON` from the default assignment on line 77 if neither `ndjsonFlag` nor `markdownFlag` are set. The block can be removed without changing behavior. Alternatively, consider making the flags mutually exclusive and returning an error if multiple are set.

### [LOW-2] `escapeMarkdown` only escapes pipe characters

**File:** `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/format/format.go:13-15`

```go
func escapeMarkdown(s string) string {
    return strings.ReplaceAll(s, "|", "\\|")
}
```

This handles the table-breaking character `|`, which is the critical one. Newlines in data could also break table formatting. If any field value could contain `\n`, consider replacing those too. For the current data model (versions, licenses, etc.) this is unlikely to be an issue.

### [LOW-3] `mustEncode` is duplicated across 3 test packages

**Files:**
- `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/crates/crates_test.go:14-17`
- `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/pypi/pypi_test.go:13-16`
- `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/internal/provider/golang/golang_test.go:14-17`

The same `mustEncode` helper is copy-pasted in all three test files. Consider extracting to a shared `internal/testutil` package. This is minor since it is test code only.

### [LOW-4] Comment style nit: "Phase 1" / "Phase 2" comments

**Files:** `crates.go:64`, `crates.go:100`, `golang.go:58`, `golang.go:77`

These "Phase 1" / "Phase 2" comments are adequate but slightly procedural. The code structure itself is clear enough that the phase labels could be replaced with more descriptive comments about *what* each phase does. Very minor.

---

## Positive Observations

1. **Input validation** -- All three providers validate identifiers with compiled regexps before making any HTTP requests. Good.

2. **Context propagation** -- Every HTTP request uses `http.NewRequestWithContext`, ensuring proper cancellation. Good.

3. **Partial failure handling** -- The PyPI and crates providers return partial metrics when some sub-requests fail, with errors aggregated in `result.Error`. This is a thoughtful UX choice.

4. **crates.io User-Agent** -- The crates provider properly sets a `User-Agent` header as required by crates.io's API policy, and tests verify it.

5. **Go module path escaping** -- The `escapeModulePath` function correctly implements the Go module proxy case-encoding convention.

6. **Test coverage** -- Each provider has tests for success, not-found, empty input, partial failure, and edge cases (pre-release versions, null fields, extras exclusion, uppercase path escaping, major version suffix). The format tests cover all provider types individually and mixed.

7. **Registry pattern** -- Clean, minimal abstraction. No over-engineering.

---

## Verdict

**Approve.** No CRITICAL or HIGH issues. The MEDIUM items are defense-in-depth improvements and a refactoring opportunity -- none are blocking. The code is clean, well-tested, and production-ready.

  "old_string": "// dependenciesResponse represents deps.dev dependencies response.\ntype dependenciesResponse struct {\n\tNodes []struct {\n\t\tRelation string `json:\"relation\"`\n\t} `json:\"nodes\"`\n}\n\nfunc (p *Provider) fetchDependenciesCount(ctx context.Context, module, version string) (int, error) {\n\tencoded := url.PathEscape(module)\n\tu := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/v3alpha/systems/go/packages/%s/versions/%s:dependencies\", p.depsdevURL, encoded, url.PathEscape(version))\n\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, \"GET\", u, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\n\tresp, err := p.client.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tdefer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()\n\n\tif resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"deps.dev dependencies: %d %s\", resp.StatusCode, http.StatusText(resp.StatusCode))\n\t}\n\n\tvar deps dependenciesResponse\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&deps); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"decoding response: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tcount := 0\n\tfor _, n := range deps.Nodes {\n\t\tif n.Relation == \"DIRECT\" {\n\t\t\tcount++\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn count, nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// requirementsResponse represents deps.dev :requirements response.\ntype requirementsResponse struct {\n\tGo struct {\n\t\tDirectDependencies []struct {\n\t\t\tName string `json:\"name\"`\n\t\t} `json:\"directDependencies\"`\n\t} `json:\"go\"`\n}\n\nfunc (p *Provider) fetchDependenciesCount(ctx context.Context, module, version string) (int, error) {\n\tencoded := url.PathEscape(module)\n\tu := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/v3alpha/systems/go/packages/%s/versions/%s:requirements\", p.depsdevURL, encoded, url.PathEscape(version))\n\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, \"GET\", u, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\n\tresp, err := p.client.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tdefer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()\n\n\tif resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"deps.dev requirements: %d %s\", resp.StatusCode, http.StatusText(resp.StatusCode))\n\t}\n\n\tvar reqs requirementsResponse\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&reqs); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"decoding response: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn len(reqs.Go.DirectDependencies), nil\n}"

     1→# Lessons

     3→## 用語
     4→
     5→### LRU eviction (Least Recently Used eviction)
     6→
     7→キャッシュが容量上限に達したとき、**最も長い間使われていないエントリから順に削除する**方式。
     8→
     9→- LRU = Least Recently Used (最も最近使われていないもの)
    10→- eviction = 追い出し、削除
    11→- 例: 上限 100 エントリのキャッシュで 101 個目を書き込むとき、最後にアクセスされた時刻が最も古いエントリを削除して空きを作る
    12→- 対になる概念: LFU (Least Frequently Used) = 使用頻度が最も低いものを削除
    13→- repiq では TTL ベースの期限切れで十分なため Out of Scope としている
    14→

lessons.md に deps.dev API の教訓を追記し、修正をコミットします。

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01UD2k4JU7RHxj6zAwahg8TB
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/14-registries/tasks/lessons.md",
  "old_string": "- repiq では TTL ベースの期限切れで十分なため Out of Scope としている",
  "new_string": "- repiq では TTL ベースの期限切れで十分なため Out of Scope としている\n\n## バグ\n\n### deps.dev API: `:dependencies` ではなく `:requirements` を使う (Go Modules)\n\n**何が起きたか:**\nGo Modules プロバイダで deps.dev の依存関係取得に `:dependencies` エンドポイントを使用した。手動テストで 404 が返り、`dependencies_count` が常に 0 になった。\n\n**原因:**\ndeps.dev v3alpha API には Go パッケージ向けに2つの類似エンドポイントがある:\n- `/v3alpha/systems/go/packages/{pkg}/versions/{ver}:dependencies` — 存在しない (404)\n- `/v3alpha/systems/go/packages/{pkg}/versions/{ver}:requirements` — 正しいエンドポイント\n\n設計時に API ドキュメントを十分に検証せず、npm 等の他レジストリからの類推で `:dependencies` を使ってしまった。\n\n**レスポンス形式の違い:**\n当初想定していた形式:\n```json\n{\"nodes\": [{\"relation\": \"DIRECT\", \"versionKey\": {...}}]}\n```\n実際の `:requirements` の形式:\n```json\n{\"go\": {\"directDependencies\": [{\"name\": \"...\", \"requirement\": \"...\"}]}}\n```\n\n**ルール:**\n1. 外部 API を使う前に、必ず `curl` で実際のレスポンスを確認する\n2. 設計ドキュメントに書いた API エンドポイントは実装前に疎通確認する\n3. テストのモックだけで安心しない — モックは実 API と乖離している可能性がある"
}
```

```
     1→# Agent Guidelines for repiq
     2→
     3→## Commit Message Convention
     4→
     5→Follow [Conventional Commits](https://www.conventionalcommits.org/) with **required scope**.
     6→
     7→Format: `<type>(<scope>): <description>`
     8→
     9→### Types
    10→
    11→- `feat` - New feature
    12→- `fix` - Bug fix
    13→- `docs` - Documentation changes
    14→- `build` - Build system, dependencies, Nix flake
    15→- `chore` - Maintenance tasks
    16→- `ci` - CI/CD configuration
    17→- `refactor` - Code restructuring without behavior change
    18→- `test` - Adding or updating tests
    19→- `perf` - Performance improvements
    20→- `style` - Code style (formatting, semicolons, etc.)
    21→- `revert` - Reverting a previous commit
    22→
    23→### Scope
    24→
    25→Scope is **mandatory**. Use the most relevant area:
    26→
    27→- `cli` - CLI entry point, argument parsing, output formatting
    28→- `provider` - Provider interface, shared provider logic
    29→- `github` - GitHub provider
    30→- `npm` - npm provider
    31→- `pypi` - PyPI provider
    32→- `crates` - crates.io provider
    33→- `go` - Go Modules provider
    34→- `specs` - Spec documents under docs/specs/
    35→- `nix` - Nix flake, dev environment
    36→- `deps` - Dependency updates
    37→
    38→### Examples
    39→
    40→```
    41→feat(cli): add --markdown output format
    42→fix(github): handle rate limit errors gracefully
    43→docs(specs): add core epic scope document
    44→build(nix): add gitleaks pre-commit hook
    45→test(provider): add unit tests for target parsing
    46→refactor(cli): extract output formatter into separate package
    47→```
    48→
    49→### PR Titles
    50→
    51→PR titles **must** follow the same convention. Squash merges use the PR title as the commit message.
    52→
    53→```
    54→docs(specs): add CLI + GitHub provider feature spec
    55→feat(github): implement GitHub provider with metrics fetching
    56→```
    57→
    58→### Bad Examples
    59→
    60→```
    61→feat: add markdown output        # missing scope
    62→update code                      # missing type and scope
    63→fix(github): Fix bug.            # don't capitalize, don't end with period
    64→Core: CLI + GitHub プロバイダー  # PR title not following convention
    65→```
    66→

> AGENT

AGENTS.md（= CLAUDE.md）に「External API Rules」セクションを追加しました。内容:

1. 実装前に `curl` でエンドポイントの存在を確認する
2. 実際のレスポンス形式を確認してから struct やモックを書く
3. テストのモックは実 API との乖離を検出できない — 実装後は必ず手動テストする

今回の deps.dev `:requirements` vs `:dependencies` の件を具体例として末尾に記載しています。

"s3fs>=2024.10.0; extra == \"aws\""

"SQLAlchemy>=2.0.36; extra == \"postgresql\""
```

**Pattern observed:** Each entry follows the format `"package_name>=version; extra == \"extra_name\""` with:
- Package specification first (name and version constraint)
- Semicolon separator with spaces on both sides
- `extra ==` (with spaces around the operator)
- Extra name in escaped double quotes
- No trailing spaces within the string
```

# Examples from "requires_dist" Array

Based on the PyPI package data for Requests, here are the raw strings containing "extra" from the `requires_dist` field:

```
"PySocks!=1.5.7,>=1.5.6; extra == \"socks\""
"chardet<6,>=3.0.2; extra == \"use-chardet-on-py3\""
```

## Formatting Pattern Observed

The extras follow this structure:
- Package specification (name, version constraints)
- Semicolon separator
- Space after semicolon
- Conditional marker: `extra == "name"`
- Double quotes around the extra name
- Backslash-escaped quotes in the JSON representation

The base dependencies (without extras) appear as:
```
"charset_normalizer<4,>=2"
"idna<4,>=2.5"
"urllib3<3,>=1.21.1"
"certifi>=2017.4.17"
```

   136→	statsSrv := httptest.NewServer(statsMux)
   137→	defer statsSrv.Close()
   138→
   139→	p := New(pypiSrv.URL, statsSrv.URL)
   140→	result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), "nonexistent-pkg")
   141→	if err != nil {
   142→		t.Fatalf("unexpected Go error: %v", err)
   143→	}
   144→	if result.Error == "" {
   145→		t.Fatal("expected result.Error to be set for 404")
   146→	}
   147→	if result.PyPI != nil {
   148→		t.Error("expected PyPI to be nil on full error")
   149→	}
   150→}
   151→
   152→func TestFetchEmptyIdentifier(t *testing.T) {
   153→	p := New("http://unused", "http://unused")
   154→	result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), "")
   155→	if err != nil {
   156→		t.Fatalf("unexpected Go error: %v", err)
   157→	}
   158→	if result.Error == "" {
   159→		t.Fatal("expected result.Error for empty identifier")
   160→	}
   161→}
   162→
   163→func TestFetchPartialFailure(t *testing.T) {
   164→	// PyPI JSON API succeeds
   165→	pypiMux := http.NewServeMux()
   166→	pypiMux.HandleFunc("GET /pypi/partial-pkg/json", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
   167→		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{
   168→			"info": map[string]any{
   169→				"version":         "1.0.0",
   170→				"license":         "MIT",
   171→				"requires_python": ">=3.8",
   172→				"requires_dist":   []string{"dep-a>=1.0"},
   173→			},
   174→			"releases": map[string]any{
   175→				"1.0.0": []map[string]any{
   176→					{"upload_time_iso_8601": time.Now().Add(-5 * 24 * time.Hour).Format(time.RFC3339)},
   177→				},
   178→			},
   179→		})
   180→	})
   181→	pypiSrv := httptest.NewServer(pypiMux)
   182→	defer pypiSrv.Close()
   183→
   184→	// Stats API returns 500
   185→	statsMux := http.NewServeMux()
   186→	statsMux.HandleFunc("GET /api/packages/partial-pkg/recent", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
   187→		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusInternalServerError)
   188→	})
   189→	statsSrv := httptest.NewServer(statsMux)
   190→	defer statsSrv.Close()
   191→
   192→	p := New(pypiSrv.URL, statsSrv.URL)
   193→	result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), "partial-pkg")
   194→	if err != nil {
   195→		t.Fatalf("unexpected Go error: %v", err)
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Should have partial metrics
   199→	if result.PyPI == nil {
   200→		t.Fatal("expected PyPI metrics for partial failure")
   201→	}
   202→	if result.PyPI.LatestVersion != "1.0.0" {
   203→		t.Errorf("latest_version: got %q, want %q", result.PyPI.LatestVersion, "1.0.0")
   204→	}
   205→	if result.PyPI.WeeklyDownloads != 0 {
   206→		t.Errorf("weekly_downloads: got %d, want 0", result.PyPI.WeeklyDownloads)
   207→	}
   208→	if result.PyPI.MonthlyDownloads != 0 {
   209→		t.Errorf("monthly_downloads: got %d, want 0", result.PyPI.MonthlyDownloads)
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	// Should also have error
   213→	if result.Error == "" {
   214→		t.Fatal("expected result.Error for partial failure")
   215→	}
   216→	if !strings.Contains(result.Error, "downloads") {
   217→		t.Errorf("expected error to mention downloads, got %q", result.Error)
   218→	}
   219→}
   220→
   221→func TestFetchNullRequiresDist(t *testing.T) {
   222→	pypiMux := http.NewServeMux()
   223→	pypiMux.HandleFunc("GET /pypi/null-deps/json", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
   224→		// requires_dist is null
   225→		_, _ = w.Write([]byte(`{
   226→			"info": {
   227→				"version": "0.1.0",
   228→				"license": "BSD",
   229→				"requires_python": ">=3.7",
   230→				"requires_dist": null
   231→			},
   232→			"releases": {
   233→				"0.1.0": [{"upload_time_iso_8601": "` + time.Now().Add(-3*24*time.Hour).Format(time.RFC3339) + `"}]
   234→			}
   235→		}`))
   236→	})
   237→	pypiSrv := httptest.NewServer(pypiMux)
   238→	defer pypiSrv.Close()
   239→
   240→	statsMux := http.NewServeMux()
   241→	statsMux.HandleFunc("GET /api/packages/null-deps/recent", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
   242→		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{
   243→			"data": map[string]any{"last_day": 10, "last_week": 100, "last_month": 500},
   244→		})
   245→	})
   246→	statsSrv := httptest.NewServer(statsMux)
   247→	defer statsSrv.Close()
   248→
   249→	p := New(pypiSrv.URL, statsSrv.URL)
   250→	result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), "null-deps")
   251→	if err != nil {
   252→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   253→	}
   254→	if result.Error != "" {
   255→		t.Fatalf("unexpected result error: %s", result.Error)
   256→	}
   257→	if result.PyPI == nil {
   258→		t.Fatal("expected PyPI metrics to be set")
   259→	}
   260→	if result.PyPI.DependenciesCount != 0 {
   261→		t.Errorf("dependencies_count: got %d, want 0", result.PyPI.DependenciesCount)
   262→	}
   263→}
   264→
   265→func TestFetchExtrasExcluded(t *testing.T) {
   266→	pypiMux := http.NewServeMux()
   267→	pypiMux.HandleFunc("GET /pypi/extras-pkg/json", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
   268→		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{
   269→			"info": map[string]any{
   270→				"version":         "3.0.0",
   271→				"license":         "MIT",
   272→				"requires_python": ">=3.10",
   273→				"requires_dist": []string{
   274→					"core-dep>=1.0",
   275→					"another-dep<2.0",
   276→					`optional-a>=1.0; extra == "dev"`,
   277→					`optional-b>=2.0; extra == "test"`,
   278→					`optional-c>=3.0; extra == "docs"`,
   279→				},
   280→			},
   281→			"releases": map[string]any{

   283→					{"upload_time_iso_8601": time.Now().Add(-1 * 24 * time.Hour).Format(time.RFC3339)},
   284→				},
   285→			},
   286→		})
   287→	})
   288→	pypiSrv := httptest.NewServer(pypiMux)
   289→	defer pypiSrv.Close()
   290→
   291→	statsMux := http.NewServeMux()
   292→	statsMux.HandleFunc("GET /api/packages/extras-pkg/recent", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
   293→		mustEncode(w, map[string]any{
   294→			"data": map[string]any{"last_day": 100, "last_week": 1000, "last_month": 5000},
   295→		})
   296→	})
   297→	statsSrv := httptest.NewServer(statsMux)
   298→	defer statsSrv.Close()
   299→
   300→	p := New(pypiSrv.URL, statsSrv.URL)
   301→	result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), "extras-pkg")
   302→	if err != nil {

   304→	}
   305→	if result.Error != "" {

   307→	}
   308→	if result.PyPI == nil {

   310→	}
   311→	// Only 2 core deps, 3 extras excluded
   312→	if result.PyPI.DependenciesCount != 2 {
   313→		t.Errorf("dependencies_count: got %d, want 2", result.PyPI.DependenciesCount)
   314→	}
   315→}

対応完了です。

**レビュー指摘**: `countNonExtraDeps` が `"; extra =="` の固定文字列マッチだったため、`";extra=="` 等のスペーシング違いを見逃す可能性

**修正内容**:
- `strings.Contains(d, "; extra ==")` → `extraRe.MatchString(d)` （正規表現 `;\s*extra\s*==`）
- テストにスペーシングバリエーション（`";extra=="` と `";  extra  =="` ）を追加
- レビュースレッドを resolve 済み
